reasoning
This is a challenging task. I need to create a FlashAttention2 backward pass kernel optimized for Hopper architecture (H100) from what was originally an A100 (Ampere) kernel. Let me understand the problem: 1. This is a flash attention backward pass for head dimension 128 2. The inputs are Q, K, V tensors and dout (gradient of output) 3. I need to compute gradients dq, dk, dv 4. The kernel should be optimized for Hopper (H100) architecture 5. It needs to work with distributed setup (8 GPUs) Key differences between A100 (Ampere) and H100 (Hopper): - H100 has higher memory bandwidth (3.35 TB/s vs 1555 GB/s) - H100 has much higher compute throughput (1979 TFLOPS FP16 vs 312 TFLOPS) - H100 has more shared memory per SM (228 KB vs 164 KB) - H100 supports new features like WGMMA, TMA (Tensor Memory Accelerator), async copies, cluster launch For FlashAttention backward pass: 1. We need to recompute attention scores from Q, K, V 2. Compute gradients for Q, K, V using chain rule 3. Handle softmax derivatives The backward pass of flash attention is complex because: - We need to recompute attention probabilities - We need to handle the softmax Jacobian - Memory access patterns are different from forward Let me think about the optimization strategies for Hopper: 1. Use larger tile sizes due to more shared memory 2. Use WGMMA (Warp Group Matrix Multiply Accumulate) for tensor core operations 3. Use TMA for efficient memory transfers 4. Consider using async copies with shared memory 5. Adjust thread block sizes for better occupancy However, this is a very complex kernel to write from scratch. The flash attention backward pass involves: - Softmax computation and its derivative - Blocked computation across multiple dimensions - Careful memory management to avoid excessive global memory access The distributed context with 8 GPUs is important—each rank processes its own batch elements independently without cross-rank communication during the backward pass, unless gradient aggregation is needed across the batch dimension. The test inputs specify batch size 2, num_heads 32, seq_len 1024, and head_dim 128, which determines the workload distribution. Since the evaluation focuses on a single `ModelNew` class without explicit distributed coordination, I should assume standard data parallelism where each GPU handles its own batch slice. For an H100-optimized implementation, I need to account for the 228 KB shared memory per SM, which is significantly larger than previous generations and allows for more aggressive blocking strategies. The FP16 precision also affects how I structure the tensor operations and memory access patterns. I need to design a kernel that effectively manages the Q, K, V data while respecting the constraint of 255 registers per thread and leveraging the available shared memory capacity.

Now I understand the target architecture. Let me write the FlashAttention backward kernel optimized for Hopper architecture. Key optimizations for Hopper: 1. Larger shared memory (228KB vs 164KB) - can use larger tile sizes 2. Warpgroup Matrix Multiply Accumulate (WGMMA) instructions 3. Async copy (cp.async) for better memory latency hiding 4. Cluster launch for cooperative thread block arrays 5. Higher memory bandwidth (3.35 TB/s vs 1.55 TB/s) 6. Higher tensor core throughput for FP16 (1979 TFLOPS vs 312 TFLOPS) For flash attention backward: - Need to compute gradients for Q, K, V - The backward pass requires recomputing attention scores - Need to handle softmax backward carefully Let me write the optimized kernel:
assistantNow I'll create an optimized FlashAttention backward kernel for Hopper architecture. The key differences from A100 (Ampere) to H100 (Hopper) include: - 40% more shared memory (228KB vs 164KB) - 2x higher memory bandwidth (3.35 TB/s vs 1.55 TB/s) - 6.3x higher FP16 tensor core throughput - WGMMA instructions for warp-group matrix operations - Async copy capabilities for better latency hiding

0; d < HEAD_DIM; ++d) {\n s_o[row * HEAD_DIM + d] += p * s_v[col * HEAD_DIM + d];\n }\n }\n \n local_sum = warp_reduce_sum(local_sum);\n \n if (lane_id == 0) {\n s_sum[row] = row_sum + local_sum;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n }\n }\n \n // Normalize output\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n s_o[i] = s_o[i] / s_sum[row];\n }\n \n // Write output and LSE\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int col = i % HEAD_DIM;\n O[o_offset + (m_start + row) * HEAD_DIM + col] = static_cast<scalar_t>(s_o[i]);\n }\n \n for (int i = tid; i < m_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n // LSE = max + log(sum)\n LSE[lse_offset + m_start + i] = s_max[i] + logf(s_sum[i] + 1e-6f);\n }\n}\n\n// Compute D = sum(dO * O) for backward pass\ntemplate<typename scalar_t, int BLOCK_M, int HEAD_DIM>\n__global__ void flash_attn_d_kernel(\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ O,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,\n float* __restrict__ D,\n int batch_size,\n int num_heads,\n int seq_len\n) {\n const int batch_idx = blockIdx.y;\n const int head_idx = blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n const int offset = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + head_idx * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;\n const int d_offset = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n __shared__ float s_reduce[THREADS_PER_BLOCK / 32];\n \n for (int seq_idx = 0; seq_idx < seq_len; ++seq_idx) {\n float d_val = 0.0f;\n \n for (int d = tid; d < HEAD_DIM; d += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n int idx = offset + seq_idx * HEAD_DIM + d;\n float o_val = static_cast<float>(O[idx]);\n float do_val = static_cast<float>(dO[idx]);\n d_val += o_val * do_val;\n }\n \n d_val = block_reduce_sum(d_val, s_reduce);\n \n if (tid == 0) {\n D[d_offset + seq_idx] = d_val;\n }\n }\n}\n\n// Main backward pass kernel for FlashAttention\ntemplate<typename scalar_t, int BLOCK_M, int BLOCK_N, int HEAD_DIM>\n__global__ void flash_attn_bwd_kernel(\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ Q,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ K,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ V,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dQ,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dK,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dV,\n const float* __restrict__ LSE,\n const float* __restrict__ D,\n int batch_size,\n int num_heads,\n int num_kv_heads,\n int seq_len,\n float scale,\n bool causal\n) {\n const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;\n const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;\n const int block_m_idx = blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int kv_head_idx = head_idx % num_kv_heads;\n const int m_start = block_m_idx * BLOCK_M;\n const int m_end = min(m_start + BLOCK_M, seq_len);\n const int m_size = m_end - m_start;\n \n extern __shared__ float smem[];\n \n float* s_q = smem; // [BLOCK_M, HEAD_DIM]\n float* s_k = s_q + BLOCK_M * HEAD_DIM; // [BLOCK_N, HEAD_DIM]\n float* s_v = s_k + BLOCK_N * HEAD_DIM; // [BLOCK_N, HEAD_DIM]\n float* s_do = s_v + BLOCK_N * HEAD_DIM; // [BLOCK_M, HEAD_DIM]\n float* s_dq = s_do + BLOCK_M * HEAD_DIM; // [BLOCK_M, HEAD_DIM]\n float* s_lse = s_dq + BLOCK_M * HEAD_DIM; // [BLOCK_M]\n float* s_d = s_lse + BLOCK_M; // [BLOCK_M]\n float* s_p = s_d + BLOCK_M; // [BLOCK_M, BLOCK_N]\n float* s_dp = s_p + BLOCK_M * BLOCK_N; // [BLOCK_M, BLOCK_N]\n float* s_ds = s_dp + BLOCK_M * BLOCK_N; // [BLOCK_M, BLOCK_N]\n \n const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n \n // Offsets\n const int q_offset = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + head_idx * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;\n const int kv_offset = batch_idx * num_kv_heads * seq_len * HEAD_DIM + kv_head_idx * seq_len * HEAD_DIM;\n const int lse_offset = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n // Load Q tile (scaled)\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int col = i % HEAD_DIM;\n s_q[i] = scale * static_cast<float>(Q[q_offset + (m_start + row) * HEAD_DIM + col]);\n }\n \n // Load dO tile\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int col = i % HEAD_DIM;\n s_do[i] = static_cast<float>(dO[q_offset + (m_start + row) * HEAD_DIM + col]);\n }\n \n // Load LSE and D\n if (tid < m_size) {\n s_lse[tid] = LSE[lse_offset + m_start + tid];\n s_d[tid] = D[lse_offset + m_start + tid];\n }\n \n // Initialize dQ accumulator\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_dq[i] = 0.0f;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n const int BLOCKS_N = (seq_len + BLOCK_N - 1) / BLOCK_N;\n \n // Loop over K, V blocks\n for (int block_n_idx = 0; block_n_idx < BLOCKS_N; ++block_n_idx) {\n const int n_start = block_n_idx * BLOCK_N;\n const int n_end = min(n_start + BLOCK_N, seq_len);\n const int n_size = n_end - n_start;\n \n // Causal mask check\n if (causal && n_end <= m_start) continue;\n if (causal && n_start >= m_end) break;\n \n // Load K and V tiles\n for (int i = tid; i < n_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int col = i % HEAD_DIM;\n s_k[i] = static_cast<float>(K[kv_offset + (n_start + row) * HEAD_DIM + col]);\n s_v[i] = static_cast<float>(V[kv_offset + (n_start + row) * HEAD_DIM + col]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute attention scores P = softmax(Q*K^T)\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * n_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / n_size;\n const int col = i % n_size;\n \n // Apply causal mask\n if (causal && (m_start + row) < (n_start + col)) {\n s_p[i] = 0.0f;\n continue;\n }\n \n float score = 0.0f;\n #pragma unroll 8\n for (int d = 0; d < HEAD_DIM; ++d) {\n score += s_q[row * HEAD_DIM + d] * s_k[col * HEAD_DIM + d];\n }\n \n // Apply softmax\n s_p[i] = expf(score - s_lse[row]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute dP = dO * V^T\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * n_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / n_size;\n const int col = i % n_size;\n \n float dp_val = 0.0f;\n #pragma unroll 8\n for (int d = 0; d < HEAD_DIM; ++d) {\n dp_val += s_do[row * HEAD_DIM + d] * s_v[col * HEAD_DIM + d];\n }\n s_dp[i] = dp_val;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute dS = P * (dP - D)\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * n_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / n_size;\n const int col = i % n_size;\n s_ds[i] = s_p[i] * (s_dp[i] - s_d[row]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Accumulate dQ = dS * K\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int d = i % HEAD_DIM;\n \n float dq_val = 0.0f;\n for (int j = 0; j < n_size; ++j) {\n // Apply causal mask\n if (causal && (m_start + row) < (n_start + j)) continue;\n dq_val += s_ds[row * n_size + j] * s_k[j * HEAD_DIM + d];\n }\n s_dq[i] += dq_val;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute dK and dV contributions\n for (int i = tid; i < n_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int j = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int d = i % HEAD_DIM;\n \n float dk_val = 0.0f;\n float dv_val = 0.0f;\n \n for (int m = 0; m < m_size; ++m) {\n // Apply causal mask\n if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + j)) continue;\n \n float ds = s_ds[m * n_size + j];\n float p = s_p[m * n_size + j];\n \n dk_val += ds * s_q[m * HEAD_DIM + d];\n dv_val += p * s_do[m * HEAD_DIM + d];\n }\n \n // Atomic add to global dK and dV\n const int kv_idx = kv_offset + (n_start + j) * HEAD_DIM + d;\n atomicAdd(reinterpret_cast<float*>(&dK[kv_idx]), dk_val);\n atomicAdd(reinterpret_cast<float*>(&dV[kv_idx]), dv_val);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n }\n \n // Write dQ to global memory\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * HEAD_DIM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int row = i / HEAD_DIM;\n const int col = i % HEAD_DIM;\n dQ[q_offset + (m_start + row) * HEAD_DIM + col] = static_cast<scalar_t>(s_dq[i]);\n }\n}\n\n// Main backward function dispatcher\ntemplate<typename scalar_t>\nvoid flash_attn_bwd_impl(\n const scalar_t* Q, const scalar_t* K, const scalar_t* V, const scalar_t* dO,\n scalar_t* dQ, scalar_t* dK, scalar_t* dV,\n int batch_size, int num_heads, int num_kv_heads, int seq_len, int head_dim,\n float scale, bool causal, cudaStream_t stream\n) {\n // Allocate temporary buffers\n float* LSE = nullptr;\n float* D = nullptr;\n scalar_t* O = nullptr;\n \n size_t lse_size = batch_size * num_heads * seq_len * sizeof(float);\n size_t o_size = batch_size * num_heads * seq_len * head_dim * sizeof(scalar_t);\n \n cudaMalloc(&LSE, lse_size);\n cudaMalloc(&D, lse_size);\n cudaMalloc(&O, o_size);\n \n // Forward pass to compute LSE and O\n const int BLOCKS_M = (seq_len + kBlockM - 1) / kBlockM;\n \n dim3 fwd_grid(BLOCKS_M, num_heads, batch_size);\n size_t fwd_smem = (kBlockM + kBlockN + kBlockN + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM * kBlockN + kBlockM + kBlockM) * sizeof(float);\n \n flash_attn_fwd_kernel<scalar_t, kBlockM, kBlockN, kBlockK><<<fwd_grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, fwd_smem, stream>>>(\n Q, K, V, O, LSE, batch_size, num_heads, num_kv_heads, seq_len, scale, causal\n );\n \n // Compute D\n dim3 d_grid(num_heads, batch_size);\n flash_attn_d_kernel<scalar_t, kBlockM, kBlockK><<<d_grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(\n O, dO, D, batch_size, num_heads, seq_len\n );\n \n // kBlockM * kBlockN) * sizeof(float);\n \n flash_attn_bwd_kernel<scalar_t, kBlockM, kBlockN, kBlockK><<<bwd_grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bwd_smem, stream>>>(\n Q, K, V, dO, dQ, dK, dV, LSE, D, batch_size, num_heads, num_kv_heads, seq_len, scale, causal\n );\n \n // Free temporary buffers\n cudaFree(LSE);\n cudaFree(D);\n cudaFree(O);\n}\n\n// PyTorch interface functions\nvoid flash_attn_bwd_fp16_impl(\n const at::Tensor& Q, const at::Tensor& K, const at::Tensor& V, const at::Tensor& dO,\n at::Tensor& dQ, at::Tensor& dK, at::Tensor& dV,\n int batch_size, int num_heads, int blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int kv_head_idx = head_idx % num_kv_heads;\n const int m_start = block_m_idx * kBlockM;\n const int m_size = min(kBlockM, seq_len - m_start);\n \n // Shared memory layout\n extern __shared__ float smem[];\n float* s_q = smem; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_k = s_q + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockN, kHeadDim]\n float* s_v = s_k + kBlockN * kHeadDim; // [kBlockN, kHeadDim]\n float* s_o = s_v + kBlockN * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_lse = s_o + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM]\n float* s_sum = s_lse + kBlockM; // [kBlockM]\n \n const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n \n // Tensor offsets\n const int q_stride = num_heads * seq_len * head_dim;\n const int kv_stride = num_kv_heads * seq_len * head_dim;\n const int q_base = batch_idx * q_stride + head_idx * seq_len * head_dim;\n const int kv_base = batch_idx * kv_stride + kv_head_idx * seq_len * head_dim;\n const int o_base = q_base;\n const int lse_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n // Initialize output and LSE\n for (int i = tid; i < kBlockM * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_o[i] = 0.0f;\n }\n \n for (int i = tid; i < kBlockM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_lse[i] = -INFINITY;\n s_sum[i] = 0.0f;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Load Q tile\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n const int q_idx = q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d;\n s_q[i] = scale * static_cast<float>(Q[q_idx]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Loop over K, V tiles\n const int num_kv_tiles = (seq_len + kBlockN - 1) / kBlockN;\n \n for (int kv_tile = 0; kv_tile < num_kv_tiles; ++kv_tile) {\n const int n_start = kv_tile * kBlockN;\n const int n_size = min(kBlockN, seq_len - n_start);\n \n // Causal mask\n if (causal && n_start >= m_start + m_size) break;\n if (causal && n_start + n_size <= m_start) continue;\n \n // Load K and V tiles\n for (int i = tid; i < n_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int n = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n const int k_idx = kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d;\n s_k[i] = static_cast<float>(K[k_idx]);\n s_v[i] = static_cast<float>(V[k_idx]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute attention scores with online softmax\n for (int m = 0; m < m_size; ++m) {\n float row_max = s_lse[m];\n float row_sum = s_sum[m];\n \n // Compute scores for this row\n for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n // Causal check\n if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + n)) continue;\n \n float score = 0.0f;\n for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) {\n score += s_q[m * kHeadDim + d] * s_k[n * kHeadDim + d];\n }\n \n // Online softmax update\n float new_max = fmaxf(row_max, score);\n float scale_old = expf(row_max - new_max);\n float p = expf(score - new_max);\n \n // Update accumulator\n for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) {\n float old_o = s_o[m * kHeadDim + d];\n s_o[m * kHeadDim + d] = old_o * scale_old + p * s_v[n * kHeadDim + d];\n }\n \n row_max = new_max;\n row_sum = row_sum * scale_old + p;\n }\n \n // Reduce within warp\n __shared__ float smem_max[kBlockM];\n __shared__ float smem_sum[kBlockM];\n \n float local_max = row_max;\n float local_sum = row_sum;\n \n #pragma unroll\n for (int offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {\n local_max = fmaxf(local_max, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, local_max, offset));\n local_sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, local_sum, offset);\n }\n \n if ((tid & 31) == 0) {\n smem_max[m] = local_max;\n smem_sum[m] = local_sum;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n if (tid < 32) {\n s_lse[m] = smem_max[m];\n s_sum[m] = smem_sum[m];\n }\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n }\n \n // Normalize and write output\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n float val = s_o[i] / (s_sum[m] + 1e-6f);\n O[o_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d] = static_cast<scalar_t>(val);\n }\n \n // Write LSE\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n LSE[lse_base + m_start + i] = s_lse[i] + logf(s_sum[i] + 1e-6f);\n }\n}\n\n// D computation kernel\ntemplate<typename scalar_t>\n__global__ void flash_attn_d_kernel(\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ O,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,\n float* __restrict__ D,\n int batch_size, int num_heads, int seq_len\n) {\n const int batch_idx = blockIdx.y;\n const int head_idx = blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int d_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n for (int s = 0; s < seq_len; ++s) {\n float d_val = 0.0f;\n \n for (int d = threadIdx.x; d < kHeadDim; d += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int idx = base + s * kHeadDim + d;\n float o_val = static_cast<float>(O[idx]);\n float do_val = static_cast<float>(dO[idx]);\n d_val += o_val * do_val;\n }\n \n d_val = block_reduce_sum(d_val);\n \n if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n D[d_base + s] = d_val;\n }\n }\n}\n\n// Backward pass kernel\ntemplate<typename scalar_t>\n__global__ void flash_attn_bwd_kernel(\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ Q,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ K,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ V,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dQ,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dK,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dV,\n const float* __restrict__ LSE,\n const float* __restrict__ D,\n int batch_size, int num_heads, int num_kv_heads, int seq_len,\n float scale, bool causal\n) {\n const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;\n const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;\n const int block_m_idx = blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int kv_head_idx = head_idx % num_kv_heads;\n const int m_start = block_m_idx * kBlockM;\n const int m_size = min(kBlockM, seq_len - m_start);\n \n // Shared memory\n extern __shared__ float smem[];\n float* s_q = smem; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_k = s_q + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockN, kHeadDim]\n float* s_v = s_k + kBlockN * kHeadDim; // [kBlockN, kHeadDim]\n float* s_do = s_v + kBlockN * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_dq = s_do + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_lse = s_dq + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM]\n float* s_d = s_lse + kBlockM; // [kBlockM]\n float* s_p = s_d + kBlockM; // [kBlockM, kBlockN]\n float* s_dp = s_p + kBlockM * kBlockN; // [kBlockM, kBlockN]\n \n const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n \n // Tensor offsets\n const int q_stride = num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int kv_stride = num_kv_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int q_base = batch_idx * q_stride + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int kv_base = batch_idx * kv_stride + kv_head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int lse_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n // Load Q tile (scaled)\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n s_q[i] = scale * static_cast<float>(Q[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d]);\n }\n \n // Load dO tile\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_lse[i] = LSE[lse_base + m_start + i];\n s_d[i] = D[lse_base + m_start + i];\n }\n \n // Initialize dQ\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n static_cast<float>(K[kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d]);\n s_v[i] = static_cast<float>(V[kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute attention scores\n kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n const int q_idx = q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d;\n s_q[i] = scale * static_cast<float>(Q[q_idx]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Loop over K, V tiles\n const int num_n_tiles = (seq_len + kBlockN - 1) / kBlockN;\n \n for (int n_tile = 0; n_tile < num_n_tiles; ++n_tile) {\n const int n_start = n_tile * kBlockN;\n const int n_end = min(n_start + kBlockN, seq_len);\n const int n_size = n_end - n_start;\n \n // Causal mask: skip if all positions are masked\n if (causal && n_start >= m_end) break;\n if (causal && n_end <= m_start) continue;\n \n // Load K and V tiles\n for (int i = tid; i < n_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int n = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n const int kv_idx = kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d;\n s_k[i] = static_cast<float>(K[kv_idx]);\n s_v[i] = static_cast<float>(V[kv_idx]);\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Process each query row\n for (int m = 0; m < m_size; ++m) {\n float row_max = s_max[m];\n float row_sum = s_sum[m];\n \n // Each thread computes scores for different K positions\n for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n // Causal check for this specific position\n if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + n)) {\n s_score[n] = -INFINITY;\n continue;\n }\n \n // Compute Q*K^T dot product\n float score = 0.0f;\n for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) {\n score += s_q[m * kHeadDim + d] * s_k[n * kHeadDim + d];\n }\n s_score[n] = score;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Find max score for this row\n float thread_max = -INFINITY;\n for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n thread_max = fmaxf(thread_max, s_score[n]);\n }\n \n // Reduce max across threads\n float block_max = warp_reduce_max(thread_max);\n if (warp_id == 0) {\n block_max = warp_reduce_max(block_max);\n }\n \n // Broadcast new max\n if (tid == 0) {\n s_max[m] = fmaxf(s_max[m], block_max);\n }\n __syncthreads();\n \n float new_max = s_max[m];\n float scale_factor = expf(row_max - new_max);\n \n // Scale existing accumulator\n for (int d = tid; d < kHeadDim; d += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_o[m * kHeadDim + d] *= scale_factor;\n }\n \n // Scale row_sum\n row_sum *= scale_factor;\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n // Compute softmax probabilities and accumulate\n float thread_sum = 0.0f;\n for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n float p = expf(s_score[n] - new_max);\n s_score[n] = p; // Store probability for later use\n thread_sum += p;\n \n // Accumulate weighted V\n for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) {\n float v = s_v[n * kHeadDim + d];\n // Atomic add simulation - just direct add since threads process different n\n atomicAdd(&s_o[m * kHeadDim + d], p * v);\n }\n }\n \n // Reduce sum across threads\n float block_sum = warp_reduce_sum(thread_sum);\n if (warp_id == 0) {\n block_sum = warp_reduce_sum(block_sum);\n }\n \n if (tid == 0) {\n s_sum[m] = row_sum + block_sum;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n }\n }\n \n // Normalize and write output\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i % kHeadDim;\n float val = s_o[i] / (s_sum[m] + 1e-6f);\n O[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d] = static_cast<scalar_t>(val);\n }\n \n // Write LSE\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n LSE[lse_base + m_start + i] = s_max[i] + logf(s_sum[i] + 1e-6f);\n }\n}\n\n// D computation kernel\ntemplate<typename scalar_t>\n__global__ void flash_attn_d_kernel(\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ O,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,\n float* __restrict__ D,\n int batch_size, int num_heads, int seq_len\n) {\n const int batch_idx = blockIdx.y;\n const int head_idx = blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int d_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n __shared__ float s_reduce[4]; // For warp reduction\n \n for (int s = 0; s < seq_len; ++s) {\n float d_val = 0.0f;\n \n for (int d = threadIdx.x; d < kHeadDim; d += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int idx = base + s * kHeadDim + d;\n float o_val = static_cast<float>(O[idx]);\n float do_val = static_cast<float>(dO[idx]);\n d_val += o_val * do_val;\n }\n \n // Reduce across thread block\n d_val = warp_reduce_sum(d_val);\n \n const int lane_id = threadIdx.x % WARP_SIZE;\n const int warp_id = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;\n \n if (lane_id == 0) {\n s_reduce[warp_id] = d_val;\n }\n __syncthreads();\n \n if (warp_id == 0) {\n d_val = (lane_id < 4) ? s_reduce[lane_id] : 0.0f;\n d_val = warp_reduce_sum(d_val);\n }\n \n if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n D[d_base + s] = d_val;\n }\n }\n}\n\n// Backward pass kernel\ntemplate<typename scalar_t>\n__global__ void flash_attn_bwd_kernel(\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ Q,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ K,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ V,\n const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dQ,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dK,\n scalar_t* __restrict__ dV,\n const float* __restrict__ LSE,\n const float* __restrict__ D,\n int batch_size, int num_heads, int num_kv_heads, int seq_len,\n float scale, bool causal\n) {\n const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;\n const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;\n const int m_block_idx = blockIdx.x;\n \n if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;\n \n const int kv_head_idx = head_idx % num_kv_heads;\n const int m_start = m_block_idx * kBlockM;\n const int m_end = min(m_start + kBlockM, seq_len);\n const int m_size = m_end - m_start;\n \n // Shared memory with alignment\n extern __shared__ __align__(sizeof(float)) unsigned char smem_buf[];\n float* smem = reinterpret_cast<float*>(smem_buf);\n \n float* s_q = smem; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_k = s_q + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockN, kHeadDim]\n float* s_v = s_k + kBlockN * kHeadDim; // [kBlockN, kHeadDim]\n float* s_do = s_v + kBlockN * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_dq = s_do + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM, kHeadDim]\n float* s_lse = s_dq + kBlockM * kHeadDim; // [kBlockM]\n float* s_d = s_lse + kBlockM; // [kBlockM]\n float* s_p = s_d + kBlockM; // [kBlockM, kBlockN]\n float* s_dp = s_p + kBlockM * kBlockN; // [kBlockM, kBlockN]\n \n const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n \n // Tensor offsets\n const int q_stride = num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int kv_stride = num_kv_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int q_base = batch_idx * q_stride + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int kv_base = batch_idx * kv_stride + kv_head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;\n const int lse_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;\n \n // Load Q tile (scaled)\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int m = i / kHeadDim;\n const int d = i THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_lse[i] = LSE[lse_base + m_start + i];\n s_d[i] = D[lse_base + m_start + i];\n }\n \n // Initialize dQ accumulator\n for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n s_dq[i] = 0.0f;\n }\n \n __syncthreads();\n \n const int num_n_tiles = (seq_len + kBlockN - 1) / kBlockN;\n \n // Process each K, V tile\n for (int n_tile = 0; n_tile < num_n_tiles; ++n_tile) {\n const int n_start = n_tile * kBlockN;\n const int n_end = min(n_start + kBlockN, seq_len);\n const int n_size = n_end - n_start;\n \n // Causal mask checks\n if (causal && n_start >= m_end) break;\n if (causal && n_end <= m_start) continue;\n \n // Load K and V tiles\n for (int i = tid; i < n_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {\n const int n = i / kHeadDim;\n // Reduce max across threads\n float block_max = warp_reduce_max(thread_max);\n if (warp_id == 0) {\n block_max = warp_reduce_max(block_max);\n }\n \n // Broadcast new max\n if (tid == 0) {\n s_max[m] Hmm, let me look at the shared memory size calculation: ```cpp const size_t bwd_smem = (kBlockM + kBlockN + kBlockN + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM * kBlockN + kBlockM * kBlockN) * sizeof(float); ``` Let me count: - s_q: kBlockM = 16 - s_k: kBlockN = 16 - s_v: kBlockN = 16 - s_do: kBlockM = 16 - s_dq: kBlockM = 16 - s_lse: kBlockM = 16 - s_d: kBlockM = 16 - s_p: kBlockM * kBlockN = 256 - s_dp: kBlockM * kBlockN = 256 Total = 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 256 + 256 = 624 floats But wait, s_q is [kBlockM, kHeadDim] = 16 * 128 = 2048 floats, not just 16! I see the problem - my shared memory size calculation is wrong! Let me fix this. The actual shared memory layout is: - s_q: kBlockM * kHeadDim = 16 * 128 = 2048 - s_k: kBlockN * kHeadDim = 16 * 128 = 2048 - s_v: kBlockN * kHeadDim = 16 * 128 = 2048 - s_do: kBlockM * kHeadDim = 16 * 128 = 2048 - s_dq: kBlockM * kHeadDim = 16 * 128 = 2048 - s_lse: kBlockM = 16 - s_d: kBlockM = 16 - s_p: kBlockM * kBlockN = 16 * 16 = 256 - s_dp: kBlockM * kBlockN = 16 * 16 = 256 Total = 2048 + 2048 + 2048 + 2048 + 2048 + 16 + 16 + 256 + 256 = 10784 floats = 43,136 bytes That's still well under 228KB, so shared memory size shouldn't be the issue... But the smem size calculation in my code is completely wrong: ```cpp const size_t bwd_smem = (kBlockM + kBlockN + kBlockN + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM + kBlockM * kBlockN + kBlockM * kBlockN) * sizeof(float); ``` This calculates: (16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 256 + 256) * 4 = 2496 bytes But the actual needed is 10784 * 4 = 43,136 bytes! reasoning
The misaligned address error is still occurring. This could be due to: 1. AtomicAdd on a pointer that's not properly aligned (fp16/bf16 tensors) 2. Incorrect memory access patterns The problem is that I'm using `atomicAdd` on `float*` cast of fp16/bf16 memory, which won't work correctly. I need to handle the atomic adds differently. For fp16, I should use `atomicAdd` with proper casting or use a separate float buffer for accumulation. Let me rewrite the kernel to use float buffers for dK and dV accumulation, then convert back.

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.cpp_extension import load_inline

cuda_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <cuda.h>
#include <cuda_fp16.h>
#include <cuda_bf16.h>
#include <c10/cuda/CUDAStream.h>
#include <c10/util/BFloat16.h>
#include <ATen/ATen.h>

constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 128;
constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
constexpr int kBlockM = 16;
constexpr int kBlockN = 16;
constexpr int kHeadDim = 128;

__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_reduce_sum(float val) {
    #pragma unroll
    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
        val += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, val, offset);
    }
    return val;
}

__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_reduce_max(float val) {
    #pragma unroll
    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
        val = fmaxf(val, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, val, offset));
    }
    return val;
}

template<typename scalar_t>
__global__ void flash_attn_fwd_kernel(
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ Q,
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ K,
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ V,
    scalar_t* __restrict__ O,
    float* __restrict__ LSE,
    int batch_size, int num_heads, int num_kv_heads, int seq_len,
    float scale, bool causal
) {
    const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;
    const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;
    const int m_block_idx = blockIdx.x;
    
    if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;
    
    const int kv_head_idx = head_idx % num_kv_heads;
    const int m_start = m_block_idx * kBlockM;
    const int m_end = min(m_start + kBlockM, seq_len);
    const int m_size = m_end - m_start;
    
    extern __shared__ float smem[];
    
    float* s_q = smem;
    float* s_k = s_q + kBlockM * kHeadDim;
    float* s_v = s_k + kBlockN * kHeadDim;
    float* s_o = s_v + kBlockN * kHeadDim;
    float* s_max = s_o + kBlockM * kHeadDim;
    float* s_sum = s_max + kBlockM;
    float* s_score = s_sum + kBlockM;
    
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int lane_id = tid % WARP_SIZE;
    const int warp_id = tid / WARP_SIZE;
    
    const int q_stride = num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int kv_stride = num_kv_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int q_base = batch_idx * q_stride + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int kv_base = batch_idx * kv_stride + kv_head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int lse_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;
    
    for (int i = tid; i < kBlockM * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) s_o[i] = 0.0f;
    for (int i = tid; i < kBlockM; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) { s_max[i] = -INFINITY; s_sum[i] = 0.0f; }
    __syncthreads();
    
    for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
        const int m = i / kHeadDim;
        const int d = i % kHeadDim;
        s_q[i] = scale * static_cast<float>(Q[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d]);
    }
    __syncthreads();
    
    const int num_n_tiles = (seq_len + kBlockN - 1) / kBlockN;
    
    for (int n_tile = 0; n_tile < num_n_tiles; ++n_tile) {
        const int n_start = n_tile * kBlockN;
        const int n_end = min(n_start + kBlockN, seq_len);
        const int n_size = n_end - n_start;
        
        if (causal && n_start >= m_end) break;
        if (causal && n_end <= m_start) continue;
        
        for (int i = tid; i < n_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
            const int n = i / kHeadDim;
            const int d = i % kHeadDim;
            const int kv_idx = kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d;
            s_k[i] = static_cast<float>(K[kv_idx]);
            s_v[i] = static_cast<float>(V[kv_idx]);
        }
        __syncthreads();
        
        for (int m = 0; m < m_size; ++m) {
            float row_max = s_max[m];
            float row_sum = s_sum[m];
            
            for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
                if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + n)) { s_score[n] = -INFINITY; continue; }
                float score = 0.0f;
                for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) score += s_q[m * kHeadDim + d] * s_k[n * kHeadDim + d];
                s_score[n] = score;
            }
            __syncthreads();
            
            float thread_max = -INFINITY;
            for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) thread_max = fmaxf(thread_max, s_score[n]);
            float block_max = warp_reduce_max(thread_max);
            if (warp_id == 0) block_max = warp_reduce_max(block_max);
            if (tid == 0) s_max[m] = fmaxf(s_max[m], block_max);
            __syncthreads();
            
            float new_max = s_max[m];
            float scale_factor = expf(row_max - new_max);
            for (int d = tid; d < kHeadDim; d += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) s_o[m * kHeadDim + d] *= scale_factor;
            row_sum *= scale_factor;
            __syncthreads();
            
            float thread_sum = 0.0f;
            for (int n = tid; n < n_size; n += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
                float p = expf(s_score[n] - new_max);
                s_score[n] = p;
                thread_sum += p;
                for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) atomicAdd(&s_o[m * kHeadDim + d], p * s_v[n * kHeadDim + d]);
            }
            
            float block_sum = warp_reduce_sum(thread_sum);
            if (warp_id == 0) block_sum = warp_reduce_sum(block_sum);
            if (tid == 0) s_sum[m] = row_sum + block_sum;
            __syncthreads();
        }
    }
    
    for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
        const int m = i / kHeadDim;
        const int d = i % kHeadDim;
        O[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d] = static_cast<scalar_t>(s_o[i] / (s_sum[m] + 1e-6f));
    }
    for (int i = tid; i < m_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) LSE[lse_base + m_start + i] = s_max[i] + logf(s_sum[i] + 1e-6f);
}

template<typename scalar_t>
__global__ void flash_attn_d_kernel(
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ O, const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO,
    float* __restrict__ D, int batch_size, int num_heads, int seq_len
) {
    const int batch_idx = blockIdx.y;
    const int head_idx = blockIdx.x;
    if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;
    
    const int base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int d_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;
    __shared__ float s_reduce[4];
    
    for (int s = 0; s < seq_len; ++s) {
        float d_val = 0.0f;
        for (int d = threadIdx.x; d < kHeadDim; d += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
            const int idx = base + s * kHeadDim + d;
            d_val += static_cast<float>(O[idx]) * static_cast<float>(dO[idx]);
        }
        d_val = warp_reduce_sum(d_val);
        const int lane_id = threadIdx.x % WARP_SIZE;
        const int warp_id = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
        if (lane_id == 0) s_reduce[warp_id] = d_val;
        __syncthreads();
        if (warp_id == 0) { d_val = (lane_id < 4) ? s_reduce[lane_id] : 0.0f; d_val = warp_reduce_sum(d_val); }
        if (threadIdx.x == 0) D[d_base + s] = d_val;
    }
}

template<typename scalar_t>
__global__ void flash_attn_bwd_kernel(
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ Q, const scalar_t* __restrict__ K, const scalar_t* __restrict__ V,
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ dO, scalar_t* __restrict__ dQ, float* __restrict__ dK_float, float* __restrict__ dV_float,
    const float* __restrict__ LSE, const float* __restrict__ D,
    int batch_size, int num_heads, int num_kv_heads, int seq_len, float scale, bool causal
) {
    const int batch_idx = blockIdx.z;
    const int head_idx = blockIdx.y;
    const int m_block_idx = blockIdx.x;
    if (batch_idx >= batch_size || head_idx >= num_heads) return;
    
    const int kv_head_idx = head_idx % num_kv_heads;
    const int m_start = m_block_idx * kBlockM;
    const int m_end = min(m_start + kBlockM, seq_len);
    const int m_size = m_end - m_start;
    
    extern __shared__ float smem[];
    float* s_q = smem;
    float* s_k = s_q + kBlockM * kHeadDim;
    float* s_v = s_k + kBlockN * kHeadDim;
    float* s_do = s_v + kBlockN * kHeadDim;
    float* s_dq = s_do + kBlockM * kHeadDim;
    float* s_lse = s_dq + kBlockM * kHeadDim;
    float* s_d = s_lse + kBlockM;
    float* s_p = s_d + kBlockM;
    float* s_dp = s_p + kBlockM * kBlockN;
    
    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const int q_stride = num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int kv_stride = num_kv_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int q_base = batch_idx * q_stride + head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int kv_base = batch_idx * kv_stride + kv_head_idx * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    const int lse_base = batch_idx * num_heads * seq_len + head_idx * seq_len;
    
    for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
        const int m = i / kHeadDim, d = i % kHeadDim;
        s_q[i] = scale * static_cast<float>(Q[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d]);
        s_do[i] = static_cast<float>(dO[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d]);
        s_dq[i] = 0.0f;
    }
    for (int i = tid; i < m_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
        s_lse[i] = LSE[lse_base + m_start + i];
        s_d[i] = D[lse_base + m_start + i];
    }
    __syncthreads();
    
    const int num_n_tiles = (seq_len + kBlockN - 1) / kBlockN;
    for (int n_tile = 0; n_tile < num_n_tiles; ++n_tile) {
        const int n_start = n_tile * kBlockN;
        const int n_end = min(n_start + kBlockN, seq_len);
        const int n_size = n_end - n_start;
        if (causal && n_start >= m_end) break;
        if (causal && n_end <= m_start) continue;
        
        for (int i = tid; i < n_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
            const int n = i / kHeadDim, d = i % kHeadDim;
            s_k[i] = static_cast<float>(K[kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d]);
            s_v[i] = static_cast<float>(V[kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d]);
        }
        __syncthreads();
        
        for (int i = tid; i < m_size * n_size; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
            const int m = i / n_size, n = i % n_size;
            if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + n)) { s_p[i] = 0.0f; s_dp[i] = 0.0f; continue; }
            float score = 0.0f;
            for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) score += s_q[m * kHeadDim + d] * s_k[n * kHeadDim + d];
            s_p[i] = expf(score - s_lse[m]);
            float dp = 0.0f;
            for (int d = 0; d < kHeadDim; ++d) dp += s_do[m * kHeadDim + d] * s_v[n * kHeadDim + d];
            s_dp[i] = dp;
        }
        __syncthreads();
        
        for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
            const int m = i / kHeadDim, d = i % kHeadDim;
            float dq_val = 0.0f;
            for (int n = 0; n < n_size; ++n) {
                if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + n)) continue;
                float ds = s_p[m * n_size + n] * (s_dp[m * n_size + n] - s_d[m]);
                dq_val += ds * s_k[n * kHeadDim + d];
            }
            s_dq[m * kHeadDim + d] += dq_val;
        }
        __syncthreads();
        
        for (int i = tid; i < n_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
            const int n = i / kHeadDim, d = i % kHeadDim;
            float dk_val = 0.0f, dv_val = 0.0f;
            for (int m = 0; m < m_size; ++m) {
                if (causal && (m_start + m) < (n_start + n)) continue;
                float p = s_p[m * n_size + n], dp = s_dp[m * n_size + n];
                float ds = p * (dp - s_d[m]);
                dk_val += ds * s_q[m * kHeadDim + d];
                dv_val += p * s_do[m * kHeadDim + d];
            }
            const int kv_idx = kv_base + (n_start + n) * kHeadDim + d;
            atomicAdd(&dK_float[kv_idx], dk_val);
            atomicAdd(&dV_float[kv_idx], dv_val);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    
    for (int i = tid; i < m_size * kHeadDim; i += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
        const int m = i / kHeadDim, d = i % kHeadDim;
        dQ[q_base + (m_start + m) * kHeadDim + d] = static_cast<scalar_t>(s_dq[i]);
    }
}

template<typename scalar_t>
__global__ void convert_float_kernel(const float* src, scalar_t* dst, int size) {
    for (int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < size; i += blockDim.x * gridDim.x)
        dst[i] = static_cast<scalar_t>(src[i]);
}

template<typename scalar_t>
void run_flash_attn_bwd(
    const scalar_t* Q, const scalar_t* K, const scalar_t* V, const scalar_t* dO,
    scalar_t* dQ, scalar_t* dK, scalar_t* dV, int batch_size, int num_heads, int num_kv_heads, int seq_len,
    float scale, bool causal, cudaStream_t stream
) {
    const size_t lse_size = batch_size * num_heads * seq_len * sizeof(float);
    const size_t o_size = batch_size * num_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim * sizeof(scalar_t);
    const size_t kv_size = batch_size * num_kv_heads * seq_len * kHeadDim;
    
    float *LSE, *D, *dK_float, *dV_float;
    scalar_t* O;
    cudaMalloc(&LSE, lse_size);
    cudaMalloc(&D, lse_size);
    cudaMalloc(&O, o_size);
    cudaMalloc(&dK_float, kv_size * sizeof(float));
    cudaMalloc(&dV_float, kv_size * sizeof(float));
    
    const int num_m_blocks = (seq_len + kBlockM - 1) / kBlockM;
    dim3 grid(num_m_blocks, num_heads, batch_size);
    const size_t fwd_smem = (kBlockM * kHeadDim + kBlockN * kHeadDim * 2 + kBlockM * kHeadDim + kBlockM * 2 + kBlockN) * sizeof(float);
    
    flash_attn_fwd_kernel<<<grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, fwd_smem, stream>>>(Q, K, V, O, LSE, batch_size, num_heads, num_kv_heads, seq_len, scale, causal);
    
    dim3 d_grid(num_heads, batch_size);
    flash_attn_d_kernel<<<d_grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(O, dO, D, batch_size, num_heads, seq_len);
    
    cudaMemsetAsync(dK_float, 0, kv_size * sizeof(float), stream);
    cudaMemsetAsync(dV_float, 0, kv_size * sizeof(float), stream);
    
    const size_t bwd_smem = (kBlockM * kHeadDim + kBlockN * kHeadDim * 2 + kBlockM * kHeadDim * 2 + kBlockM * 2 + kBlockM * kBlockN * 2) * sizeof(float);
    flash_attn_bwd_kernel<<<grid, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bwd_smem, stream>>>(Q, K, V, dO, dQ, dK_float, dV_float, LSE, D, batch_size, num_heads, num_kv_heads, seq_len, scale, causal);
    
    const int convert_blocks = (kv_size + 255) / 256;
    convert_float_kernel<<<convert_blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(dK_float, dK, kv_size);
    convert_float_kernel<<<convert_blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(dV_float, dV, kv_size);
    
    cudaFree(LSE); cudaFree(D); cudaFree(O); cudaFree(dK_float); cudaFree(dV_float);
}

void flash_attn_bwd_fp16(const at::Tensor& Q, const at::Tensor& K, const at::Tensor& V, const at::Tensor& dO, at::Tensor& dQ, at::Tensor& dK, at::Tensor& dV, int b, int h, int kh, int s, int hd, float scale, bool causal) {
    run_flash_attn_bwd<c10::Half>(Q.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), K.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), V.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), dO.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), dQ.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), dK.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), dV.data_ptr<c10::Half>(), b, h, kh, s, scale, causal, c10::cuda::getCurrentCUDAStream());
}

void flash_attn_bwd_bf16(const at::Tensor& Q, const at::Tensor& K, const at::Tensor& V, const at::Tensor& dO, at::Tensor& dQ, at::Tensor& dK, at::Tensor& dV, int b, int h, int kh, int s, int hd, float scale, bool causal) {
    run_flash_attn_bwd<c10::BFloat16>(Q.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), K.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), V.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), dO.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), dQ.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), dK.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), dV.data_ptr<c10::BFloat16>(), b, h, kh, s, scale, causal, c10::cuda::getCurrentCUDAStream());
}
"""

cpp_source = """
void flash_attn_bwd_fp16(const at::Tensor& Q, const at::Tensor& K, const at::Tensor& V, const at::Tensor& dO, at::Tensor& dQ, at::Tensor& dK, at::Tensor& dV, int b, int h, int kh, int s, int hd, float scale, bool causal);
void flash_attn_bwd_bf16(const at::Tensor& Q, const at::Tensor& K, const at::Tensor& V, const at::Tensor& dO, at::Tensor& dQ, at::Tensor& dK, at::Tensor& dV, int b, int h, int kh, int s, int hd, float scale, bool causal);
"""

flash_attn_ext = load_inline(name='flash_attn_hopper', cpp_sources=cpp_source, cuda_sources=cuda_source, functions=['flash_attn_bwd_fp16', 'flash_attn_bwd_bf16'], with_cuda=True, extra_cuda_cflags=['-O3', '-arch=sm_90'], verbose=False)

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_heads, num_kv_heads, head_dim, dropout=0.0, causal=True):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.scale = 1.0 / (head_dim ** 0.5)
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.causal = causal

    def forward(self, q, k, v, dout):
        b, h, s, d = q.shape
        _, kh, _, _ = k.shape
        dq, dk, dv = torch.zeros_like(q), torch.zeros_like(k), torch.zeros_like(v)
        if q.dtype == torch.bfloat16:
            flash_attn_ext.flash_attn_bwd_bf16(q, k, v, dout, dq, dk, dv, b, h, kh, s, d, self.scale, self.causal)
        elif q.dtype == torch.float16:
            flash_attn_ext.flash_attn_bwd_fp16(q, k, v, dout, dq, dk, dv, b, h, kh, s, d, self.scale, self.causal)
        else:
            flash_attn_ext.flash_attn_bwd_fp16(q.half(), k.half(), v.half(), dout.half(), dq.half(), dk.half(), dv.half(), b, h, kh, s, d, self.scale, self.causal)
            dq, dk, dv = dq.to(q.dtype), dk.to(q.dtype), dv.to(q.dtype)
        return dq, dk, dv
